| 088 | <a id="slice-diag.9c"></a>**DIAG.9c** | Done | Rare-failure minimization, observer-effect experiments and qualified optional rr/TTD escalation; DG15. **Reverse-debugging and watchpoint routes landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed): `crd-diag-corruption-specimen` (in the default build on every lane) writes one word past four cells into a sentinel; `scripts/reverse-debug-recipes.py` runs the specimen uninstrumented first (a corrupt run that does not detect is `SPECIMEN_INVALID`, an instrumented run that disagrees is `OBSERVER_EFFECT`), then a route, and passes only when the route names `crd_diag_corrupt_neighbour` at the marked store (reverse) or inside the writer (forward watchpoint); a clean run with a writer, a missing tool, a refused permission, an ineligible host, missing symbols, a lost write, a debugger internal error, a timeout and a trace over its byte bound each fail with their own verdict, recorded with tool version, permissions, both outcomes, instruction and byte bounds. Routes: `gdb-record` (process record with a bounded log, live `reverse-continue` to `watch -l`, `record save` and an offline replay that bisects the restored log with `record goto`; gdb 15.1's internal error on a restored log's `continue`/watchpoints is avoided, not hidden) found at the store on linux-gcc-debug and linux-gcc-shipping, and declared at configure time not a route on sanitizer builds (`record save` never finishes the reserved shadow space); `gdb-watch` (hardware watchpoint) found on all four Linux lanes; `cdb-watch` (cdb from the WinDbg package, `ba w8`, `sxd av` for MSVC ASan's shadow faults) found on win-debug, win-shipping, win-clang-cl-shipping and win-asan, local evidence only; `ttd` reports `PERMISSION_DENIED` unelevated; `rr` reports `HOST_INELIGIBLE` on WSL2 (no cpu PMU, paranoid 2, never lowered). Clean controls pass on every route; the 2,000-instruction control reports the write out of the log. 34 mocked verdict tests (`crd-diag-reverse-debug-recipes`, a repository CI step on both runners); CI installs and records `gdb`; teeth shown (the off-by-one repaired, any reverse stop taken as the writer, truncation not distinguished, any cdb stop taken as the writer). **Seed, choice and input minimization landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed): `crd/core/minimize.hpp` holds the DIAG.4b greedy remover promoted (`minimize_sequence`, 1-minimal), `bisect_toward` and `minimize_toward_zero`, under an all-K-runs oracle (a candidate that fails on some runs only is a race, counted `flaky` and never kept; a candidate is never judged on fewer than K runs; a run budget stops with a result that still reproduces, not claimed minimal; an input that does not reproduce is left as given). Choices: the schedule-script minimization runs through it (K = 3) and still yields `{test.reacquired, fp.finalizing}` (local only: `crd-jobs-schedcheck-tests` is in no hosted job). Seed and hits: `minimize_fault_plan` (crd-perf) narrows each rule's range by bisection, pins the plan to the hits it fires on (seed 0, every rule always firing) and removes single hits or rules to a 1-minimal plan; shown on synthetic programs (every-allocation plan to its one needed hit, a seeded four-rule plan to its two needed hits on two sites), on a real injector through a `FaultAllocator`, and on the real cooker (a 40% read plan whose cook recovered from two faults shrinks to the one `.meta` read that fails it, each kept hit checked needed). Inputs: `minimize_record_inputs` (crd-ceir-cook) shrinks a failed plan record's arguments and read values toward 0 while the run fails at the same op, cuts the read stream to the reads the failing run made, and writes a new record that `replay.run` reproduces (1000 to 10 and -1000 to -1 on a two-switch program, a seeded `random_demo.ceir` failure to `main(k + 1)` with its earlier draws 0); refused before anything runs exactly where `replay.run` refuses, external I/O included. Teeth shown (any-of-K, no precondition, no pass restart, bisection stopping a step early, no upper-bound narrowing, a pinned plan keeping its seed, the failure compared without its op, the external-I/O refusal skipped). **Observer-effect experiments landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed): instrumentation levels are off (no flight recorder: the natural run), basic and full; basic and full are a new `FlightPolicy::detail` (authored `detail = "full"` or `"basic"`, default full), basic recording Warning and above only, counting the informational events it leaves out (`total_filtered()`, `flight.status` `filtered`) and firing every trigger; the CFR1 record carries the level in its former must-be-zero pad byte (earlier records read as full, an unknown level is refused, version and fuzz corpus unchanged). `run_observer_experiment` (crd-perf) runs a reproduction K times per level and compares the caller's failure signature, never a time: the natural outcome is kept beside every capture; verdicts `consistent`, `not-reproduced`, `natural-unstable`, `observer-effect` (naming the level, including a level that fails in only some runs) and `instrument-failure` (a level that reproduced without a capture, or a refused policy); each level's run times are reported as its cost and judged by nothing. Consistent on a frame-budget failure through `FlightFrameWatch` (every lane), an allocation-failure plan on a real injector behind a `FaultAllocator` (basic keeps the fault and drops the frames, full keeps both) and the real cooker failed by its first unrecovered read fault (both captures name the file); `instrument-failure` on a `notrigger` plan and on a policy masking the hitch trigger; `observer-effect` shown only on reproductions perturbed on purpose and labelled synthetic (no real observer effect found). Found and fixed: crd-toml took the last value of a key defined twice in a table, so a diagnostic asset's second `capacity` silently won; it now refuses duplicate keys (TOML forbids them; every committed TOML file parses unchanged). Teeth shown (basic not filtering, an unknown level decoded, the natural outcome not kept, a missed capture passing, any-of-K agreement, the asset ignoring `detail`, duplicate keys accepted, the injected-fault event at Info). **Remaining (before the 2026-10-12 CI read):** one elevated TTD recording (user); rr qualification on a host with hardware counters (none available; explicit capability limit until then); the hosted run, which must show `crd-diag-reverse-debug-recipes` and the repository step green on both runners, `crd-diag-reverse-debug-gdb-watch` and `-gdb-watch-clean` on all four Linux lanes, `-gdb-record`, `-gdb-record-clean` and `-gdb-record-incomplete` on linux-gcc-debug and linux-gcc-shipping, the gdb version in the package census, `crd-diag-corruption-specimen` building on every lane, and the `[diag9c][minimize]` cases green on all six lanes in crd-core-tests (5), crd-perf-tests (5, the real-injector case answering `not-reproduced` on the shipping lanes), crd-ceir-cook-tests (3) and crd-resources-tests (1, its shipping branch on the shipping lanes), the `[diag9c][observer]` cases green on all six lanes in crd-perf-tests (5; the real-injector case answering `not-reproduced` on the shipping lanes, the frame-budget case consistent everywhere) and crd-resources-tests (1, `not-reproduced` on the shipping lanes), the `detail` asset cases in crd-perf-tests' `[assets]` cases, the duplicate-key case in crd-toml-tests, and the bundle corpus replay unchanged. **2026-10-12 CI read:** the hosted clause is shown in complete run [38236026326](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38236026326) (push) at `7a958b88`: `crd-diag-reverse-debug-recipes` and the "Reverse debugging verdicts" step pass on both runners, the gdb watch routes pass on every Linux lane, the gdb record routes on the gcc debug and shipping lanes, gdb 15.1 is in the package census, and the 14 `[minimize]` and 6 `[observer]` cases, the duplicate-key case and the bundle corpus replay are green on all six lanes. **TTD recorded (2026-10-14):** the user's elevated recordings, re-judged with `--replay-trace`, answer `FOUND: crd_diag_corrupt_neighbour at corruption_specimen.cpp:78` (corrupt) and `CLEAN` (clean). Getting there fixed the recipe: the reverse replay now steps back one instruction past the data breakpoint's stop (it had stopped on line 79), the packaged TTD inside the WinDbg app is reported as such (it cannot be launched even elevated; the standalone `Microsoft.TimeTravelDebugging` package is), and a recording can be re-judged unelevated; 35 recipe tests, the new ones failing on the old recipe ([TTD session](sessions/2026-10-14-diag-9c-ttd-recording.md)). rr stays an explicit capability limit: this host's WSL2 exposes no hardware performance counters (`HOST_INELIGIBLE`). **Remaining:** the hosted run of the recipe change (`crd-diag-reverse-debug-recipes` and the repository step on both runners); [audit](sessions/2026-10-06-needs-ci-audit.md#rows-closed-on-7a958b88-2026-10-12). **Done (2026-10-15):** complete-tier run [38640651640](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38640651640) (workflow_dispatch) at `56748127` carries the recipe change: the "Reverse debugging verdicts" step runs 35 tests OK on both runners, `crd-diag-reverse-debug-recipes` passes on all six lanes, and the gdb watch routes on every Linux lane and the gdb record routes on linux-gcc-debug and linux-gcc-shipping pass again; the other clauses were shown in 38236026326. rr stays an explicit capability limit until a host with hardware performance counters exists; [audit](sessions/2026-10-06-needs-ci-audit.md#rows-closed-on-56748127-2026-10-15). **Owner** (2026-10-25, [DIAG.12](#slice-diag.12)): rr on a host with hardware performance counters is [QUAL.2](#slice-qual.2)'s. | [DIAG.9b](#slice-diag.9b) | [Detail](design/runtime-diagnostics.md#diag-9c); | 089 | <a id="slice-diag.10a"></a>**DIAG.10a** | Done | Stateful production-path fuzzing for allocators, diagnostics, current commands/reload and IR differentials; DG16. **Allocator operation sequences landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-allocator-fuzzing.md)): `crd-fuzz-memory-allocators` (`tests/foundation/memory/fuzz`, harness and corpus replay CTest on every lane, libFuzzer on `linux-clang-fuzz`) drives TLSF, pool, linear, stack, the growable TLSF, pool and linear, the offset allocator, the epoch ring, the `DiagnosticAllocator` over TLSF and the `SlotMap` with decoded operation streams against an independent model (no overlap, alignment, ownership, intact payloads, exact capacity models, `validate_structure()`, statistics back to zero, offset coalescing, parent leak check, identical outcome trace on a second run); only contract-valid operations reach raw allocators, malformed sizes and alignments are refused, and the checked layers take double, interior, foreign and evicted frees, redzone overruns, writes into quarantine and stale, double-erased and forged handles. Seven defects found and fixed, each kept as a corpus input (five as written seeds, two as adopted libFuzzer inputs): TLSF `try_allocate` asserted on a non-power-of-two alignment; TLSF in-place reallocation never adjusted `bytes_in_use`; the linear, stack and growable linear allocators drove `bytes_in_use` past zero by alignment padding; the `DiagnosticAllocator` passed interior pointers and evicted double frees to its backing heap and named the discarded block in its saturation report; a TLSF pool whose capacity is not a multiple of 16 put its end sentinel at a misaligned address (UBSan, found by libFuzzer); `OffsetAllocator::reset()` kept node neighbour links, so the next whole-span free coalesced with regions that no longer existed (found by libFuzzer). Every `crd_fuzz_target()` now has a `fuzz.py` entry, enforced by `test-repository-tools.py` (the bundle target had none); the replay driver is headless, so an engine assertion fails a replay instead of hanging it on a dialog. Four bounded libFuzzer rounds (4 workers); the last ran 587 s, 256,345 executions, no artifact; two reports were the harness's own (an offset span no bin represents cannot be handed out whole by design; an evicted pointer is ambiguous while a saturation passthrough block is live) and the harness was corrected. Teeth: twelve engine breaks and one registry break each fail their seed or test. Corpus 23 inputs (17 written, 6 adopted). **The crd-perf evidence readers landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-evidence-readers.md)): three more targets in `tests/foundation/perf/fuzz` with corpora under `corpus/<reader>/` (the bundle corpus moved to `corpus/bundle/`), each in `fuzz.py`: `crd-fuzz-perf-flight` (`decode_flight_record`; an accepted record must be one a `FlightRecorder` can write, restated independently in the harness, and re-encode to its own bytes), `crd-fuzz-perf-assets` (the flight-policy and fault-plan assets and the fault-plan text over crd-toml; a refusal leaves the output untouched, an accepted policy or plan round-trips through its canonical form) and `crd-fuzz-perf-capture` (`validate_capture_buffer` and `CaptureView` against a reference CPROF layout the harness computes from the header). Seed functions check every seed's verdict as they write it (`VerdictSink`). Findings fixed: the flight-record decoder accepted self-contradicting records (an unarmed trigger, suppressed kinds without a count, an end reason the post window contradicts, a ringless capture with events, gaps across the trigger, a clip flag on a short message, Info under Basic, an unavailable replay without a reason); the CPROF reader trusted every offset behind its section sizes (a name-blob size that wraps the section sum, unchecked sample arrays, blob slots, offsets and terminators, unterminated names, unknown counter kinds and types, counts past the profiler's tables and a frame record's arrays, an unaligned buffer); crd-toml had no nesting bound (a 20,000-deep array overflowed the stack) and clamped silently (an integer past the 64-bit range read as `INT64_MAX`, a float overflow as infinity, a number token past 63 characters cut short), now refused past 64 levels and out of range; libFuzzer found the Perfetto export's span `end - begin` overflowing `i64` (UBSan), now computed without overflow (adopted input `6ea98773...`). No on-disk layout or record version changed. Single-process libFuzzer rounds: flight 24.7 M executions, assets 0.9 M, capture 2.9 M after the fix, all clean; teeth: 25 deliberate breaks, each failing its seed or case. Open: under four concurrent processes, two perf targets (the unchanged bundle target too) showed input-independent register and stack corruption that no artifact reproduces; single-process runs are clean; the cause is not established (recorded in the session). **Run records landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-run-records.md)): `crd-fuzz-ceir-record` (`tests/execution/ceir-cook/fuzz`, in `fuzz.py`; crd-ceir-cook's module now owns the harness directory) decodes each input as given and again with its header's payload size and checksum recomputed, so mutated payloads reach the payload reader; an accepted record must be one `replay.record`, `record_host_run` or the device recorder can write (restated in the harness), re-encode to its own bytes, name exactly the missing inputs its states name and show no divergence from a trace that repeats it, and the decoder may allocate at most eight times the input plus 64 KiB. Findings fixed in `decode_record`: it reserved arrays from counts before checking the bytes left (a record under 500 bytes reserved 3 MB of events, 1.5 MB of reads or 16 MB of device words), and it accepted records no recorder writes: an input state its need contradicts, program, build or arguments not needed-and-recorded, external results recorded, a schedule recorded off the host executor or missing on a host record, a device tolerance recorded off the device executor or missing on a device record, a seam input recorded when only possibly needed or past the 65,536-read bound, a kept read of an input said not needed, a cancelled run, a kernel whose text does not hash to its recorded hash, device kernels or buffers in a plan or host record; `replay.prepare` presented such claims as available inputs. Such record files are now refused at `replay.run` and `replay.prepare` as not decoding; the DIAG.9b external-results cases were split accordingly (the file route refused as malformed, `record_missing_inputs` asserted on the same claims in memory, honest needed-and-missing records still refused as missing), the device case for edited kernel text checks the decoder and the in-memory replay, two ceir-host fixtures that turned a host record into a plan record now store the schedule as the plan recorder does, and a new check covers the unheld-stream rule the corpus cannot reach (a stream past 65,536 reads exceeds the 64 KiB input bound). No layout or schema changed. Corpus 57 seeds (8 honest records, 30 one-way forgeries, 19 structural); 14 teeth each fail the corpus replay or the new check. **Command decoding landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-command-decoding.md)): `crd-fuzz-ceridc-diag` (`tests/tools/ceridc/fuzz`, in `fuzz.py`; the ceridc module owns the harness directory) feeds ceridc's stdio MCP transport line by line (the owned JSON parser, `mcp_handle`, the diag tool's decoding, `DiagCommandService::execute` with every command ceridc binds and the sandbox's flight and fault commands, under a host line's grant and an optional file root that does not exist; lines that could reach ceridc's file verbs are not fed), plus native requests of raw bytes. Oracles: an independent iterative RFC 8259 and UTF-8 validator agreeing with the parser within its limits, JSON-RPC replies (one line of valid UTF-8, -32700, -32600 with a null id, no reply to a notification, the id echoed exactly), the diag tool's decoding restated with its report equal byte for byte to the native answer for the page it names, the service's refusal order restated (no handler runs on a refusal; a command's check answers only bad-argument or unavailable), document fields equal to the typed result, page bounds, a walk to the end of every snapshot with repeatable cursors, and no allocation left live. Findings fixed: the JSON parser accepted numbers outside the grammar (`+1`, `01`, `.5`, `1.`), read a token past 63 characters as its first 63, turned an overflow into infinity and accepted ill-formed UTF-8 (now the RFC grammar read whole by `std::from_chars`, out of range and ill-formed UTF-8 refused; `crd/core/utf8.hpp`); `as_i64` cast doubles past the i64 range (undefined; now the default); the MCP transport answered string ids as 0 and rounded or truncated others, silently dropped a batch array or bare value, compared method and tool names as C strings cut at a NUL, cut string arguments past 511 bytes or at a NUL to another path, read a non-boolean `dry_run` as false (a `"true"` string wrote the file) and cast frame counts and translations from any double (ids now echo exactly or the request is -32600; arguments are used as sent or the call is -32602); crd-perf's and crd-asset-io's JSON writers passed ill-formed UTF-8 through and the service cut its command echo inside a sequence (now `\ufffd` and a boundary clip), so a document is valid UTF-8 whatever bytes a request or evidence held; libFuzzer found a refusal's typed cursor left at 0 while its document named the request's (now every typed field matches). Corpus 52 seeds; 20 teeth each fail the corpus replay or a unit case; three single-process libFuzzer rounds of 600 s (the last 123,357 executions, no artifact). The commands' own file readers open files outside crd-core's I/O fault seam, so which paths they open is not observed (the target's root does not exist). **Resource reload landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-resource-reload.md)): `crd-fuzz-resources-reload` (`tests/assets/resources/fuzz`, in `fuzz.py`; the resources module owns the harness directory) writes each pack an input names to a per-process temp file and drives the real `ResourceManager`: raw pack bytes (read by `crdr_read`, then mounted, loaded, reloaded, evicted, loaded again, unmounted) or a script over two packs (built through `CrdrWriter` and overridden with lying ranges, duplicated ids, torn tails, compressed or dropped chunks; raw bytes; in-place corruption; mount, unmount, `load_sync`, release, `reload_mount_now`, drain to a zero budget, pin). Oracles: a restated CRDR layout and pack rule set (zstd decoding the compressed chunks), an allocation bound on `crdr_read`, and a model of the manager compared after every operation with `find_entry`, `entry_count`, `mount_count`, `current_memory_use` and every held handle; a payload reaches only its own type's loader; every byte is returned. Findings fixed: `crdr_read` reserved what compressed chunks claimed before decoding them (a 96-byte file reserved 1 TiB; two claims whose sum wraps reserved 16 bytes and decoded 32 into them) and kept a zero-filled tail when a frame decoded short (claims now bounded by `crdr_max_decompressed_size` and decoded exactly); `do_reload_mount` copied a torn pack's offsets into the live table before any range check and checked reloaded ranges with a sum that wraps, reloaded an id a newer mount served from the older pack, swapped a retyped entry's payload into a block of the old type (freed by the wrong loader), ignored added ids, kept dropped ids at stale offsets, swapped a duplicated id twice and never re-accounted the resident size; `unmount` erased ids an older mount still provided, contrary to its header; a re-issue after eviction crossed types; a manifest naming one id twice mounted. A pack is now taken whole or not at all by mount and both reload paths (unreadable, unparsable, torn or a duplicated id refuses it), the live table serves each id from the newest mount naming it, and an id keeps its type across reload and re-issue (`resource_manager.hpp`). The replay driver ends on a Debug CRT report instead of a modal dialog. Corpus 34 seeds; 12 teeth each fail the corpus replay; two single-process libFuzzer rounds of 600 s (152,294 and 106,198 executions) found nothing further. **The compiled and reference CEIR comparison landed locally** (2026-10-10, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-10-diag-10a-ceir-differential.md)): `crd-fuzz-ceir-differential` (`tests/execution/ceir/fuzz`, in `fuzz.py`) decodes every input to a generated program inside the cook's module-validity set (arith, core control flow, the three cell kinds, calls with recursion, async and task tokens, data-parallel bodies, the input ops; up to four functions, a step budget of 64 to 131,072, a scripted host input or none) and runs it through the reference interpreter and the compiled plan, which must agree on the error class, values, every cell, every map output and the op an error blames (stable id); only the documented compile refusal (a stateful or input-reading parallel body) is accepted, and the plan run twice, a fresh reference session and the printed and serialized twins must give the same outcome. Findings fixed in both tiers: the reference charged every terminator a step, so the tiers ran out of steps at different ops (a step is now a dispatched non-terminator op, a loop iteration, a parallel-range index or a fold step; `plan::run` takes `max_steps`); unbounded recursion overflowed the native stack (on win-asan near 190 nesting levels, 180 through a `map_reduce` body), now `NestingTooDeep` at `exec::kMaxNesting` = 64 in both tiers, blamed on the same op, a parallel body's sub-interpreter starting at its parent's level (the value appended to both error enums; `kLastExecError`/`kLastRunError` bound the run-record decoder); a parallel range's `hi - lo` and indices overflowed i64 (a full-i64 range by 2^62 came out empty), the reference reserved a map slot per index before running any body (16 GiB for a 2^31-index range) and an instruction-free body was unbounded (each index and fold step is now a step, counted in unsigned arithmetic by `exec::parallel_range_count`); a `for` step past INT64_MAX overflowed `iv += step`; the compiled `parallel_for`/`map_reduce` re-entered from its own body mixed the inner activation's entries into the outer map; libFuzzer found that the reference lost the map of a parallel op inside a parallel body (its sub-interpreter was discarded; the parent now adopts it) that the plan's per-body token scope moved the whole run-wide token store (quadratic; now a base index), and that both tiers gave each parallel body a fresh copy of the budget, so a body re-entering the program ran for minutes (a parallel body's steps are now the run's). Eight `[diag10a]` cases in crd-ceir-tests pin the agreed answers. Corpus 35 (32 seeds, 3 adopted inputs; the slowest replays in about 2 s under ASan); 15 teeth each fail the corpus replay, a unit case or libFuzzer's 10 s limit (the two overflow teeth only through the unit cases on MSVC, and as UBSan reports on `linux-clang-fuzz`). **The host provider's parallel range landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10a-host-parallel-range.md)): the crd-jobs provider counts and indexes a range with `exec::parallel_range_count`/`parallel_range_index` (it computed `hi - lo` in i64 and cut the count to 32 bits: `[INT64_MIN, INT64_MAX)` by 2^62 came out empty, 2^32 + 3 indices ran as 3, 2^33 as none); its budget rule is now stated and tested: what the submitting thread runs spends the run's budget as in the reference (one step per index, spent with the new `Interpreter::spend_fuel(steps)` before any per-index slot is sized, so a range past the budget is FuelExhausted on the parallel op with nothing allocated, and the map_reduce fold, which ran on one uncharged `sub_fuel` budget), while each body a pool worker runs keeps its per-item `sub_fuel`; a range index's body, a fold step and a pooled launch body start at the level of the op that runs them (`Interpreter::set_nesting`, the level carried with the job), so they stop at `kMaxNesting` on the op the reference blames (each started at level 0); worker bodies run on Medium fibers, because on win-asan a body past about 35 levels faulted on its 64 KiB Small fiber's guard page and ended the process with no report (34 levels fitted, 36 did not); each body's scratch starts with a 64 KiB chunk instead of the 256 MiB default (256 indices took 16.8 s on win-debug, now 0.27 s). Six `[ceir][host][diag10a]` cases in `crd-ceir-host-tests` compare the provider with the reference on the same module (nesting parity for the three body kinds, every level usable on a worker, the whole-line range, the budget refusal with no allocation, the fold budget, the submitting thread's remaining budget equal to the reference's); eight teeth each fail them (the Small-fiber tooth crashes win-asan; it passes on win-debug, which fits 62 levels in 64 KiB). The provider is not in a fuzz target: per-item budgets, the NoSemantics refusal of a parallel op inside a body and launch errors raised at await make its agreement with the sequential tiers a filtered oracle. **The virtual-memory and streaming allocators landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10a-vm-streaming-allocators.md)): two more kinds of `crd-fuzz-memory-allocators` (appended, so every committed input keeps its kind), each input reserving at most 16 MiB and releasing it with the allocator. The `VirtualMemoryAllocator` is held to an exact model of its bump offset and committed high-water mark (whole commit blocks capped at the reservation, purge to the page holding the live top, recommitted bytes reading zero, the tail poisoned under ASan, in-place reallocation only of the top block); the `StreamingAllocator` runs under an input-scripted residency policy (shed the category, any category, one block, nothing, or claim bytes never freed) with every category's charge tracked exactly, the budget held on every success, the retry bound (17 consultations) and no consultation for a request nothing can serve. Findings fixed: the arena's `try_allocate` asserted on a non-power-of-two alignment; a commit block that is a page multiple but not a power of two was rounded with the power-of-two mask, and one too large for any page multiple wrapped to one page; a reallocate of the top block asking for a stricter alignment returned it in place, misaligned; a request no resident heap can serve (non-power-of-two alignment, larger than the reservation, aligned past any chunk) reached the eviction loop, so the policy shed resident data for it; `GrowableTlsfAllocator::try_allocate` took a new chunk from its parent for a non-power-of-two alignment and then refused it (a chunk left behind per request, committed for good under a virtual-memory parent); the streaming budget checked the request but charged the heap's block, so rounding carried a category past its budget (it now holds the charge, sheds for a block rounded past the remaining budget and refuses, without consulting the policy, a block larger than the whole budget). One `[memory][streaming][diag10a]` case in `crd-memory-tests` pins the last refusal. Corpus 37 (31 written, 6 adopted); 11 teeth each fail a seed or the case (one, the arena's commit left unpoisoned, on win-asan). **The unknown-origin CEIR and CKIR artifacts reinvestigated locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10a-unknown-origin-artifacts.md)): the four loader targets gained cross-format oracles (an accepted CEIR text module must serialize, deserialize and print back to the same text; an accepted CEIR blob must print to text the parser accepts and prints identically; an accepted CKIR blob must write `.ckir` text that reads back to the same blob), the contracts `binary.hpp` and `ckir_asset.hpp` already state. On that harness the six artifacts adopted by REPO.DEV.11 (`553e7ecf`, `d85628dd`, `183a3e2e`, `e18eb14e`, `34ce93c0`, `c7c59902`) each replay clean alone, 10,000 times in one process under ASan and UBSan with stack-use-after-return and strict string checks, and under valgrind memcheck on linux-gcc-debug; none reproduces its round-four symptom, so they stay labelled unknown. The new oracles found real defects, each fixed with corpus inputs: the CEIR binary loader accepted names the text form cannot express (an op name `tes).sink`, which `183a3e2e` carries, a trait `2rd`, any struct, enum, param, trait, field, case, symbolic-dim, type-class or attr-class name outside `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.]*`, an empty symbol ref) and a type attribute naming no type, whose printing asserted in `type_of`; the CEIR text reader refused the zero-member `!tuple<>` and `!variant<>` the type model, the factories and the printer allow; the CKIR blob loader accepted out-of-range op, dtype, kind, tier, statement, scope, stage, depth and interpolation bytes (which index past the text writer's name tables), a rank past 8, dims past the rank and stray stage-output slots, and the `.ckir` reader refused the INT64_MIN its writer emits. Ten CEIR and seven CKIR written seeds, each checked refused as it is written, four adopted libFuzzer findings and two adopted inputs that crashed only inside a libFuzzer process (see below); one `[diag10a]` case in `crd-ceir-tests`; twelve teeth each fail a corpus or the case. The corruption of unknown origin recurred twice in that batch in single libFuzzer processes (a UBSan object-size report on `Array<u8>`'s container annotation in `crd-fuzz-ceir-text`, an ASan SEGV writing address 4 at the end of `asan_annotate_live_range` in `crd-fuzz-ckir-binary`) and once in a 10,000-repetition replay (a container overflow on the byte `push_back` had just annotated); each input replays clean (30,000 and 328,000 further repetitions), a known-answer CPU probe was clean (about 150,000 passes in WSL, 64,000 on Windows) and Windows logged `cl.exe` access violations at ten different offsets in 30 days. **The container-overflow control ran locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10a-container-overflow-control.md)): with `detect_container_overflow=0` (shown to disable the annotation in this runtime) libFuzzer processes paired by seed, run count and moment faulted 7 times in 12 against 8 in 12 with the default (about one event per 1.5 M and 1.0 M executions), one of them inside `asan_annotate_live_range` itself, so the annotation path is where the corruption is noticed, not where it is made. Two bounded follow-up slots showed that neither libFuzzer, the sanitizers nor the input is needed: the replay driver faulted 130 times in about 2.31 M executions, an unsanitized linux-gcc-debug loop faulted twice in about 4.68 M (once as a wrong result on an input it handled correctly 5,922 other times), every artifact replays clean and one equals a committed seed; `MallocAllocator::try_allocate` asserted `is_pow2` on the constant 8 its caller loads just before the call. No defect in the engine, the harness or the instruments is indicated; the rate rose about twentyfold within the session; native Windows loops (small exposure, different executables), a compile-determinism probe and the known-answer probe were clean. The 191 artifacts are counted, not adopted (a dated exception in test-instruments). **Remaining (before the 2026-10-12 CI read):** the run that separates the host from the software, on another machine or on this one booted into native Linux (the user's; No event that does not replay appeared in about 69.7 M executions on four CPU models, so the corruption of unknown origin follows the development host, not the software (whether its CPU or its WSL2 guest is not separated; nothing waits on that). The gate is per outcome: every value of 32 result/status enums must be produced at a site that ran, and every transition site of the flight recorder's window machine must have run, unless an exclusion owns it with a class and a reason; stale exclusions fail. The first measurement found 30 gaps, all now owned: 15 real untested error paths (`owned-gap`: four record-minimization verdicts, the host-executor adapter's Unavailable and BadArgument, the inspect script's NotStarted and Unfinished, HostLoad::LoadFailed, RecordWrite::Failed, a cancelled external-results analysis, the flight recorder's fatal path with the ring held, the harness's own Timeout verdict and two generation wraps with no seam), the Linux crash-record write path (`dying-child`: the dying specimen writes no profile), Win32-only results, shipping/sanitizer-only results and three resource failures no test can force; and one declared verdict, `MissingSymbolizer`, is never produced. Teeth: deleting one test's `RejectedUnknownVersion` check made the real run fail naming that value and site; an exclusion of a value that ran, a `windows-only` exclusion of a compiled site and a removed scope group each failed. `tests/support/diag/nonreproduced-failures.json` registers six flaky or nonreproduced failures with signature, reruns, status and session; `scripts/test-diag-coverage.py` (the `crd-diag-coverage` CTest and a repository CI step) covers every verdict without a build and fails if any build file, CI step or script reads the register. **Twelve owned gaps closed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10b-owned-gaps-closed.md)): `[diag10b]` cases assert the record minimizer's Unrunnable, NotReproduced, Budget and Cancelled (with `ExternalReplay::Cancelled`), the host executor adapter's Unavailable and BadArgument when called directly, `RecordWrite::Failed`, `HostLoad::LoadFailed`, `ScriptOutcome` NotStarted and Unfinished, and the harness's Timeout verdict (crd-perf-tests now builds `crd-diag-cpu-hotspot-specimen`); seven site breaks each failed a case; the measurement with the old registry was STALE naming exactly those three and PASS without them (853 CTests; regions 9,235/10,372, branches 5,989/7,644, lines 17,163/19,691), and no `owned-gap` exclusion remains. **Seeds on hosted lanes landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; it is a multi-preset `preset_steps` entry that `check-ci-tiers.py` now holds to exactly its listed presets in each job, and the verdict tests derive the hosted lanes' facts from their configure presets. The exact step command was DETECTED for every applicable seed on all five local lanes (3, 5, 3, 6, 6). (2026-10-11, found by the DIAG.10c batch's full win-debug CTest: the coverage preset made `crd-build-profiles` fail, because `test-native-build-profiles.py` pinned 24 visible configure presets; the pin is now 25, [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-numerical-mode.md).) **Remaining:** only the hosted run, which must show the `Seeded detector failures` step green with `report.json` DETECTED for every applicable seed on win-debug (3), win-asan (5), linux-gcc-debug (3), linux-gcc-asan (6) and linux-clang-tsan (6; a REGISTRY_STALE there would mean a seeded text no longer matches the hosted checkout), and `crd-diag-seeded-faults` and `crd-diag-coverage` green on all six lanes and both repository steps on both runners, crd-ceir-cook-tests and crd-ceir-host-tests green on all six lanes with their `[diag10b]` cases, and crd-core-tests, crd-jobs-tests, crd-memory-tests and crd-perf-tests (with its `[diag10b]` timeout case, and, on every lane with asserts (win-debug, win-asan, linux-gcc-debug, linux-gcc-asan, linux-clang-tsan), the two generation-wrap cases and the ring-held fatal case, which the shipping lanes compile out) green on all six lanes, including the sanitizer cases now judged against the lane promise with report evidence (heap overflow and use after free on win-asan and linux-gcc-asan, use after return and leak on linux-gcc-asan, the data race on linux-clang-tsan, the allocator poison modes on both ASan lanes). **2026-10-12 CI read:** the hosted clause is shown in complete run [38236026326](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38236026326) (push) at `7a958b88`: the `Seeded detector failures` step reports DETECTED for every applicable seed (win-debug 3, win-asan 5, linux-gcc-debug 3, linux-gcc-asan 6, linux-clang-tsan 6, no `REGISTRY_STALE`), `crd-diag-seeded-faults` and `crd-diag-coverage` pass on all six lanes, both repository steps pass on both runners, and the `[diag10b]` cases are green (7 with asserts, 4 on win-shipping). | 091 | <a id="slice-diag.10c"></a>**DIAG.10c** | Done | Numerical/type/unit/time-domain and resource-pressure sanity probes plus lifecycle/soak oracles; DG16. **Numerical-mode expectation and the jobs boundary landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-numerical-mode.md)): the declared mode is the thread-start mode (MXCSR `0x1F80`: all exceptions masked, round to nearest even, no FTZ/DAZ; x87 control word `0x027F` on Windows, `0x037F` on Linux), only control bits are compared, and `crd/core/fp_environment.hpp` reads, writes, scopes and compares it field by field (an unread field is `Unread`, never a match; the x87 word is read where GNU inline assembly exists). At the crd-jobs boundary every job starts in the declared mode whatever its thread's mode, keeps a mode it sets across its waits and migrations, and loses it when it ends. Defect found and fixed: the Linux fiber switch saved neither MXCSR nor the x87 control word (its comment called them caller-saved; the psABI makes their control bits callee-saved), so a job's flush-to-zero or rounding mode reached the worker's scheduler stack and every later job, and a waiting job resumed in another job's mode; four `[diag10c]` cases in crd-jobs-tests all failed on the old switch on linux-gcc-debug and pass with `fiber_switch_lin64.S` saving both and `fiber_init_stack` writing the declared values into a fresh 72-byte frame; [ADR-0033](decisions/0033-jobs-implementation-architecture.md) is amended. Four `[diag10c]` cases in crd-core-tests check the thread-start mode, the scoped round trip and the field-by-field comparison, and show that each compared MXCSR field changes a float result. **Value-domain probes and the generation-attributed result oracle landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-value-domains-and-result-oracle.md)): `crd/core/value_domain.hpp` declares a domain class by class (NaN, +Inf, -Inf, subnormals, -0) plus a closed numeric range (`any`, `finite` by default, `distance` admitting +Inf, `unit-interval`; an i64 `IntDomain`), and probes values from their bit patterns only, so a domain that allows a special value never reports it and no verdict depends on the caller's FTZ/DAZ/rounding (binary32 classified from its own bits and widened exactly). `crd/ceir/cook/result_oracle.hpp` judges a successful CEIR run's results against an independent reference or an integer invariant (CountMismatch, OutOfDomain, DiffersFromReference) and `ReloadSet::locate_result` attributes the first wrong result to the generation the handle names (current, retiring or gone) and the op that defined it in that generation's file; the correct generation is never blamed. Six `[diag10c]` cases in crd-core-tests and four in crd-ceir-cook-tests (a hot-swapped generation's wrong constant, a retiring plan's wrong op after the fix and after a drain, a wrapped square caught by an invariant on both executors, counts and invalid domains); twelve deliberate breaks each bit (eleven failed a case, one stopped on the debug build's bounds assertion). The CEIR executors are integer-only, so a CEIR plan run has no floating-point mode to probe. **Shape/stride, units/time-domain and float domains at the tensor and audio boundaries, and the mode probe at the audio callback and third-party calls, landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-tensor-and-audio-boundaries.md)): `crd/core/layout_probe.hpp` checks a strided view's claim (rank bound, element count and byte size in u64, first and last offsets in elements and bytes in i64 formed per dimension, named storage only when named, overlap only when proven and a violation only for a written view) and walks it against a `FloatDomain`, naming the first offender by coordinates and lane; `crd/hesap/tensor/probe.hpp` applies both to tensor views. DLPack import now refuses what the probe refuses; defects found and fixed there: a wrapping element count imported as an empty tensor, an offset past i64 imported, compact strides of an empty shape overflowed a signed product, a missing shape array was dereferenced and an address-space wrap imported; an expanded (stride-0) or empty capsule still imports. `crd/audio/audio_boundary.hpp`: a source binding declares its sample rate (0 = undeclared, unchecked, renders as before) and both renderers refuse a rate mismatch and a partial frame (defects: a ragged buffer's tail was dropped silently, a looped source of less than one frame divided by zero); `audio_sample_domain` names a non-finite sample on the bus; `render_audio_period` runs the device callback in the declared mode, keeps a mode it sets inside its period and reports both, and the WASAPI device counts divergent and non-finite periods (defect: a non-stereo endpoint got stereo written at its own channel stride; it now renders through a stereo scratch sized in `start()`). `call_keeping_fp_environment` reports and undoes a mode a foreign call leaves; a DLPack producer's deleter runs through it on every path. Sixteen deliberate breaks each failed a case (the partial-frame break was observed on two cases, one by the division-by-zero crash the fix prevents, and the missing-shape break by the access violation it prevents); the live device soak on this host ran 10 s at 48 kHz with zero divergent and zero non-finite periods. **Lifetime accounting beyond heap bytes landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-lifetime-accounting.md)): `crd/core/lifetime_ledger.hpp` counts, per class (FileHandle, LibraryHandle, MappedRegion with its bytes, DeviceObject, CallbackRegistration), acquisitions, matched releases, live, high water and live bytes with relaxed atomics in a constant-initialized table (no lock, no allocation); a release it cannot match changes nothing and is counted, so a window between two snapshots is Underflowed rather than wrapped, else Outstanding, Drained or Balanced, with per-class deltas and a one-line rendering. Wired: `vm::reserve`/`release` and the crd-jobs fiber stacks (MappedRegion), `DynamicLibrary` (LibraryHandle), `log::FileSink` open/close/rotation (FileHandle), the process-wide identity registry's Resource and Program identities (DeviceObject; Pass identities and a registry of one's own are not counted), and `EventBus` subscriptions (the bus is now move-only), `FileWatcher` entries, `ResourceManager` reload subscriptions, installed log sinks and the jobs observer (CallbackRegistration); handles opened and closed inside one call are scoped by rule and `crash.cpp` is not wired, both named in the design doc. `crd/core/os_resource_probe.hpp` is the independent oracle: whether this process holds a file (Windows: the process's own handle table, File-type disk handles by normalized final path; Linux: `/proc/self/fd` by device and inode) and how much of a range is reserved or mapped (VirtualQuery; `/proc/self/maps`), Unavailable with a reason where the OS cannot be asked. `[diag10c]` cases in crd-core-tests (five), crd-vm-tests (two), crd-jobs-tests (three: init/shutdown holds and returns exactly seven stacks, the observer, 32 jobs recording concurrently), crd-platform-tests (three), crd-log-tests (three, the file held and released as the OS sees it, also across a rotation), crd-app-tests (one), crd-resources-tests (one) and crd-gpu-context-tests (two, the ledger agreeing with the registry's `live_count`). **Queue pressure and exhausted budgets at the jobs and resource boundaries landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-queue-pressure.md)): `crd/core/budget_pressure.hpp` judges a bounded resource's `BudgetReading` (its bound or none, held now, the high water where the owner keeps it, refusals, soft-bound overruns) with integer arithmetic only: a level (Unread, Unbounded, Within, Near at 900 permille computed without an overflowing product, Full, Over) and a window verdict, worst first (Exhausted, Overrun, Saturated, Near, Relaxed only when a kept high water proves the peak below Near, else Undetermined; Unjudged when two readings cannot be compared). `crd::jobs::pressure_snapshot()` reports the counters, each fiber tier, each injection queue (no high water kept, so never Relaxed) and the pinned lanes, and `ResourceManager::memory_pressure()` the soft memory budget with its settled high water and overruns (all blocks pinned or held left it over the budget without a trace). Defects found and fixed: a full injection queue or local deque dropped the job where asserts are off, so its waiter hung; and each thread's pinned work was a single slot, so a second job pinned to a thread before it drained the first asserted or was overwritten and lost, reachable from authored CEIR with two `task.main_thread` tasks in flight (the new crd-ceir-host-tests case and a crd-jobs-tests case stopped on the assert on win-debug, and the jobs case hung on win-shipping until killed); the pinned lane is now a bounded MPMC queue (`Config::pinned_queue_capacity`) and every full queue or lane is a counted, always-on fatal ([ADR-0033](decisions/0033-jobs-implementation-architecture.md) amended). The queue-full specimen shows both refusals as Exhausted readings from inside the fatal, and the counter and fiber exhaustion specimens now also require theirs; eleven deliberate breaks each failed a case. (2026-10-11: the Visual Studio dialog that blocked reconfiguring the previous batch is gone; win-debug reconfigured normally.) **Warm/cold, soak, repeated-reload and shutdown workloads with one stressor at a time and seeded failure injection, measured slowdowns, and threads in the lifetime ledger landed locally** (2026-10-11, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-11-diag-10c-workloads.md)): `crd/core/workload_probe.hpp` runs cycles of setup, iterations and teardown (iteration 0 cold, the rest warm) and judges every iteration's result against what it should be (or cycle 0's cold reference), every seeded injected iteration (a pure function of seed, cycle and iteration; never the cold or the last one; always followed by a clean one) as detected, missed or never fired and the next as recovered or not, every cycle's lifetime-ledger window and progress against a bound; the verdict is the worst finding, located (SetupFailed, Diverged, FaultMissed, NotRecovered, IterationFailed, Leaked, Stalled, FaultNotFired, else Clean). Stressors are enumerated, and `attribute_finding` blames a variant's finding on a stressor only against a clean baseline that differs in exactly that one. `slowdown_report` classifies only from measurements (at least 8 samples a window, a baseline median of at least 1000 ns and an interquartile range within it; Slower or Faster only when the median moved past 250 permille AND the quartiles separate, else Inconclusive or Steady); hosted lanes judge results, lifetimes and progress, never speed, and the workloads print their timing verdict as evidence. Threads are the ledger's sixth class (the crd-jobs workers and hang watchdog, the asynchronous logger's worker, an inspect host's run, the diagnostic panel's request worker and the WASAPI render thread), judged against the OS by identity (thread id and start time; ended once it has exited, even unjoined). Workloads: crd-jobs-tests soaks three init/shutdown cycles of 64 parallel sums with a closed-form answer (every sum right, every window balanced, every worker the OS saw gone after its cycle; a 4-thread variant is clean, a variant with one 7 s delay is Stalled and attributed to Delay against a 5 s progress bound, the one wall-clock judgment the hosted lanes make); crd-resources-tests runs three fresh ResourceManagers through a cold load and 23 reloads each, with a memory-budget variant (clean, overruns counted) and failed-read and short-read fault-plan variants on the real I/O seam (every injected reload detected, last good mesh kept, recovered). Defect found and fixed by the jobs soak: on the default wake path a job pinned to a worker woke one arbitrary sleeper, so the target could sleep on with the job in its lane and its waiter hung (the 4-thread variant hung on win-debug; on the old scheduler a dedicated case lost the first job pinned to an idle worker); a pinned push now wakes every sleeper behind a fence, and a worker about to sleep wakes every sleeper when an announced sleeper holds pinned work ([ADR-0033](decisions/0033-jobs-implementation-architecture.md) amended). A second defect found by the new logger-thread case under linux-clang-tsan: `log::flush()` waited only for an empty queue, but the worker pops a record before it takes the sink lock, so flush could return before the last record was written; it now also waits for the record being delivered (a swept-race case: 4000 rounds of log then flush). Eighteen of twenty deliberate breaks failed a case; removing either half of the pinned-wake fix alone does not (each half closes the common case; together they close the window the other leaves). **Driver/device-reset and host-pressure evidence, the GPU side of pressure and the mode witness around driver calls landed locally** (2026-10-12, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-12-diag-10c-device-and-host-pressure.md)): `crd/core/host_pressure.hpp` reads the host's physical memory, commit charge (bounded only where the OS enforces it: Windows always, Linux in strict overcommit) and this process's resident set with its peak as `BudgetReading`s, unread with a reason where the OS cannot answer; the usable RAM agrees with a second OS interface. `IGpuContext::device_memory()` reads the OS's per-process budget and usage of the device's adapter (DX12: DXGI `QueryVideoMemoryInfo`, local and non-local; Vulkan: `VK_EXT_memory_budget`, now enabled whenever advertised), and an absent segment (WARP's non-local 0/0) stays unread, never Full; usage follows a committed 256 MiB buffer and back on the RTX 4070 Ti SUPER and on WARP through DXGI and on the RTX driver through the Vulkan budget, while llvmpipe advertises the extension but reports a usage that does not follow allocations (its test says so instead of calling it tracked). `IFrameGraph::transient_pressure()` reports the transient budget (bound, footprint, high water, one refusal per refused build) and `gpu.resources` now reports each context's device memory and each frame graph's transient budget instead of `heap_usage: "unavailable"`. `crd/gpu/driver_witness.hpp` runs instance and device creation, dxc and shaderc, `ExecuteCommandLists`/`vkQueueSubmit` and both presents under the numerical-mode witness (a mode a call leaves is put back and counted per kind with the first offender; none seen on the RTX, WARP or llvmpipe). Each backend takes the host and device pressure with the loss's sequence when it records a device lost, and `attribute_device_fault` calls a failure an EngineDefect only when the same inputs fail on another configuration (Injected for a simulated one; else PressureCoincident at a Full or Over reading, NotReproduced, or Undiscriminated). Defect found and fixed by the transient-budget cases: a second `build()` of one recording placed the transients over the first build's, leaking them on DX12 with their heaps and the device (DXGI usage stayed 32 MiB up after the graph died, and the next test's validation-enabled device creation failed) and binding memory to already-bound images on Vulkan (a 16 MiB leak on the RTX budget); a frame graph now builds once per recording on both backends and a second build without `reset()` is refused (not a budget refusal). Fourteen deliberate breaks each failed a case. **GPU queue depth, the native owners in the lifetime ledger and their shutdown landed locally** (2026-10-12, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-12-diag-10c-queue-depth-and-native-owners.md)): `crd/gpu/queue_pressure.hpp` (crd-gpu-context, device-free) counts a step in flight from the completion seam that queued it until a seam observed it; a timeline observed through a value retires its step and every earlier step on its queue (fence-less ones included), and a loss, a forgotten queue or device, or a timeline that starts again abandons, counted; a completion with nothing pending is unmatched, and a step the fixed table cannot hold leaves the reading unread. The DX12 seams (`dx12_submit`, `dx12_signal`, `dx12_wait`, every removal) and the Vulkan seam (`vk_submit`, both fence waits, both idle waits, the first loss, `vk_forget_device`) feed it; each DX12 context forgets its queue, and a private-data interface on each DX12 device ends its counts when D3D12 destroys it. `IGpuContext::queue_pressure()` (appended, default unread) reads it as an unbounded `BudgetReading`, and `gpu.resources` reports each context's `queue_*` fields and `queue_depth_read`. A synchronous context never leaves a step in flight, the DX12 deferred frame graph leaves one frame and the Vulkan graph's two-slot ring at most two. Two more ledger classes (eight, filling the u8 masks): NativeGpuObject (the DX12 device reference per device scope, created queues, fences and the swapchain; the crd-kir-dx12 backend's device, queue and fence; the Vulkan instance, device, every fence and swapchain) and OsObject (the GLFW window, every fence event, the WASAPI enumerator, endpoint, audio client, render client and period event); `LifetimeHold` records a release that is implicit. Shutdown workloads (3 DX12 and 3 Vulkan cycles of create, four round trips and destroy; window cycles; audio stop and three restarts) leave every cycle Balanced; the OS judges the window (FindWindow, the USER-object count). Defects found and fixed: a WASAPI start after stop opened a second session over the stopped one and leaked it (ten OS objects instead of five); and a DX12 device's queue counts outlived it, so a later device at the same address inherited a lost reading (three new cases failed in the whole DX12 program until the device-end watch); and a simulated Vulkan loss let the teardown that followed destroy objects in-flight work still used (lavapipe leaked a worker allocation under LeakSanitizer) until the seam drained the real device after recording a simulated loss. Twenty-two deliberate breaks each failed a case (twenty-one on win-debug, one on linux-gcc-asan). **Remaining:** only the hosted run, which must show crd-core-tests (forty `[diag10c]` cases, including the two host-pressure cases and the native-class and `LifetimeHold` case), crd-jobs-tests (eighteen, including the queue-full specimen in both modes, the concurrent pinned pushes and the workload soak under linux-clang-tsan, and the pinned idle-worker case), crd-ceir-host-tests (one), crd-ceir-cook-tests (five), crd-hesap-tensor-io-tests (five), crd-audio-tests (eight: the seven plus the device-lifetime case, whose refused start must balance on every hosted lane; plus the soak's counter and render-thread checks wherever a render endpoint exists), crd-vm-tests (two), crd-platform-tests (five: the three plus the window case and the window shutdown cycles, whose failed create must balance where no display exists), crd-log-tests (five), crd-app-tests (one), crd-resources-tests (three, the reload workload's fault variants answering FaultNotFired by design on the shipping lanes), crd-perf-ui-tests (two) and crd-gpu-context-tests (sixteen: the ledger pair, attribution, loss pressure, the driver witness and the eight queue-depth cases) green with their `[diag10c]` cases on all six lanes (including the jobs, value-domain, layout, DLPack, oracle, lifetime, pressure, workload and queue-depth cases under linux-gcc-asan and linux-clang-tsan), crd-perf-gpu-bridge-tests green with the `gpu.resources` device memory, transient and queue fields, crd-gpu-context-vulkan-tests green on the lavapipe Linux lanes with its nine `[diag10c]` cases (the memory case naming llvmpipe's untracked usage, the transient budget and second-build refusal, the witness counts, the injected loss's pressure, the instance/device/fence holds with two round trips, the one-shot clear and the two-slot frame ring, the shutdown workload, the injected loss abandoning pending frames, the present surface's swapchain and ring fences) and the updated `gpu.resources` case, and on the Windows lanes with a failed context start balancing where no Vulkan device exists, crd-gpu-context-dx12-tests green on the Windows lanes with its eleven `[diag10c]` cases on WARP (DXGI usage following a committed buffer, the transient budget and second-build refusal with the DXGI leak check, the witness counts including present where a window exists, the recorded removal's pressure, the compute context's holds and drained queue, the deferred frame graph, the present surface's holds, the shutdown workload, the destroyed device's fresh counts; the injected-loss and injected-removal cases on win-debug and win-asan, their refusal on win-shipping), crd-kir-dx12-tests green with its ledger case, the counter and fiber exhaustion specimen cases green with their pressure readings, `crd-diag-coverage` green with the numerical-mode, value-domain, layout/boundary, lifetime-ledger, budget-pressure, workload-oracle, host/device pressure and queue-depth scope groups and the FpDifference, ValueClass, ValueViolation, ResultVerdict, LayoutViolation, LayoutOverlap, SourceBindingVerdict, LifetimeVerdict, OsPresence, PressureLevel, PressureVerdict, StressorChange, SlowdownVerdict, ProgressVerdict, WorkloadVerdict, FindingAttribution and FaultAttribution outcome sets over its eleven programs, and `crd-build-profiles` green with its visible-preset pin at 25. (A hang watchdog that classifies a stall from the pressure and depth readings is a follow-up outside the clauses of this row.) **2026-10-12 CI read:** the hosted clause is shown in complete run [38236026326](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38236026326) (push) at `7a958b88`: the `[diag10c]` cases are green on all six lanes with the stated counts per suite (crd-core-tests now 41, crd-jobs-tests 18, crd-gpu-context-tests 16, crd-audio-tests 8, the other suites as listed), the nine Vulkan cases pass with assertions on lavapipe, the eleven DX12 cases and the `crd-kir-dx12-tests` ledger case on every Windows lane, and `crd-diag-coverage` and `crd-build-profiles` pass on every lane. **2026-10-14:** scheduled run [38534848594](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38534848594) failed the logger case's post-join Absent check on linux-clang-tsan: a joined Linux thread stays listed as running until the kernel finishes its exit, so `os_thread_alive` now also reads `PF_EXITING` as ended (teeth: 2 failures in 300 TSan runs without it, none in 600 with it; [session](sessions/2026-10-14-diag-10c-thread-exit-probe.md)); the next complete run must show the `[diag10c]` cases green again on linux-clang-tsan. Nothing local or user-side remains; the row waits on [DIAG.9c](#slice-diag.9c) and [DIAG.10a](#slice-diag.10a) in table order; [audit](sessions/2026-10-06-needs-ci-audit.md#rows-closed-on-7a958b88-2026-10-12). **Done (2026-10-15):** complete-tier run [38640651640](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38640651640) (workflow_dispatch) at `56748127` shows the `[diag10c]` cases green again on linux-clang-tsan (121 cases, the asynchronous logger case with 7 assertions and the thread probe case with 11) and on the other five lanes (129 on each Windows lane, the four Vulkan cases skipping where no Vulkan device exists; 121 on each Linux lane, where the twelve DX12 cases are not built); [audit](sessions/2026-10-06-needs-ci-audit.md#rows-closed-on-56748127-2026-10-15). | [DIAG.10b](#slice-diag.10b) | [Detail](design/runtime-diagnostics.md#diag-10c); [ADR-0133](decisions/0133-runtime-diagnostics-and-instrumentation.md); [run 38209499793 repairs](sessions/2026-10-12-complete-run-38209499793-repairs.md); [Thread probe reap race](sessions/2026-10-23-scheduled-runs-thread-probe-reap-race.md); [Complete run 2026-10-12](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38236026326); [Complete run 2026-10-15](https://github.com/yatiyr/CRD/actions/runs/38640651640); [Needs CI audit](sessions/2026-10-06-needs-ci-audit.md) |
| 092 | <a id="slice-diag.11a"></a>**DIAG.11a** | Done | Reusable app/headless/sandbox and installed external-consumer diagnostics with shipping/authorable policies; DG17. **The reusable host and its authored profile landed locally** (2026-10-12, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-12-diag-11a-reusable-diagnostic-host.md)): `crd::perf::DiagnosticHost` (`crd/perf/diagnostic_host.hpp`) is the sandbox's DIAG.9b flight host moved into crd-perf and widened to one lifecycle for every host: the crash handler, the flight recorder and its triggers, the bundle writer, the fault injector with the I/O and GPU seams' hooks and the flight.*/fault.* commands, undone in order by `stop()`; `sandbox/src/flight_host.*` is deleted. Its modes come from an authored `diagnostic-host` asset (`*.host.toml`; `assets/diag/host_default.host.toml` held equal to `DiagnosticHostProfile{}`, `host_investigate.host.toml` with injection and a record/inject grant): `crash_handler`, `flight` and `fault_injection` take off/on/required (on: used where the build can, reported Unavailable otherwise; required: the run is refused), plus the crash and capture directories, the flight policy, the bundle bound, the grant, `allow_remote` and the watchdog window; an unknown key or bad value is refused by name. The default is local and bounded (read-only grant, no injection, remote-enable/upload/process-memory refused without `allow_remote`). `start()` decides every mode before installing anything and refuses once the jobs pool runs, so the choice precedes the pool, devices and tools; `apply()` changes the capture directory, the bundle bound and the policy (re-read, so an edited file counts) live and answers RestartRequired, naming the fields and changing nothing, for every other field (also Busy, Refused, Unchanged, NotStarted). The hosts: `ApplicationDesc::diagnostics` (the host owns the crash handler, every `tick()` is one of its frames, `run()` gives the pool its watchdog window, an unstarted host makes the Application invalid); the sandbox (`--diag-host`, its CLI overrides held to the same rules); ceridc as the headless host (`ceridc mcp --diag-host`). A small application (`crd-diag-host-specimen`) starts the host from a profile file and fails a fatal invariant or a headless assertion, and the profile's capture directory receives a bundle naming the trigger, the events before it, the recipe, this build and the executable's own identity; through ceridc a capture pending at the end of an MCP session is written at stop and read back by `bundle.inspect`. Fifteen `[diag-host]` cases in crd-perf-tests (seven moved from crd-sandbox-inspect-tests), three `[diag11a]` Application cases in crd-app-tests (they need a window and skip headless), one real-binary case in crd-ceridc-tests; fourteen deliberate breaks each failed a case. **The installed external consumer landed locally** (2026-10-12, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-12-diag-11a-installed-consumer.md)): the package exports the diagnostic host's family (`Cerid::core`, `units`, `time`, `vm`, `log`, `containers`, `memory`, `jobs`, `toml`, `perf`, with `math` and `simd-flags`; include directories `$<BUILD_INTERFACE:...>`; an include-only partner missing from an install is refused), its symbols as `CeridConfig.cmake` declares them (`Cerid_DEBUG_INFORMATION`: MSVC compile PDBs beside their archives, DWARF in the GCC archives) and the diagnostic assets under `share/cerid/diag` (`Cerid_ASSET_DIR`). `bundle.inspect` decodes a flight record into a trigger item, a flight item and one event item per retained event (`flight_refused` with the reason when the decoder refuses it), a trigger's long source path keeps its file name, and `CRD_DIAG_INVARIANT` names its types fully. The thin application `tests/support/package/diag-consumer` (`find_package(Cerid)`, `Cerid::perf` only) is built by `test-package-consumer.py` (the `crd-package-consumer` CTest of the public-check presets) against the moved prefix after the engine build tree is deleted (the local stand-in for a clean machine), with the engine's debug information proven in the program's own PDB or DWARF; it starts the host from a profile authored from the installed assets, reports its capabilities, fails a fatal invariant on purpose and leaves a flight bundle, and its own `inspect` route names the trigger at its failing line, the forty events before it, this build and its own module. The three binaries passed: diagnostic (`win-debug`, `linux-gcc-debug`), profiling disabled (`win-shipping`, `linux-gcc-shipping`: fault injection Unavailable) and optimized profiling (`win-shipping-profile`); eleven deliberate breaks each failed, and one control (the build tree kept) passed, which shows why it is deleted. **The examples landed locally** (2026-10-12, committed, not pushed; [session](sessions/2026-10-12-diag-11a-examples.md)): allocator selection, jobs, reload and capture are routes of the same application (`example_*.cpp`; it now also links `Cerid::memory`, `Cerid::jobs` and `Cerid::toml`, the modules they name), each a fresh process started from a profile authored from the installed assets and judged on every packaged binary, its bundles read back through the application's own `inspect`: a TLSF heap or the `DiagnosticAllocator` over it handed to the owner through `watch_allocator` and exhausted into an allocation-failure bundle naming the chosen allocator (the diagnostic choice also reports a one-byte overrun, kept in the bundle before the failure); the pool configured by the host, its work checked against the known total, and a starved main-thread job captured as a hang; reload of the authored diagnostic configuration through `apply()` (unchanged, an edited policy applied live at a new generation, a changed watchdog window answered RestartRequired, a new capture directory applied live) and of the application's own table through its reader on the engine's I/O fault seam, where a seeded read failure keeps the last good generation and is captured in the new directory; three captures requested through `flight.trigger` while the application runs, the newest two kept. The five example runs passed on `win-debug`, `win-shipping`, `win-shipping-profile`, `linux-gcc-debug` and `linux-gcc-shipping`; seven application breaks and two engine breaks each failed. **All local clauses are shown**; the row waits only for its first hosted run (Needs CI once a published run carries this work), which must show crd-perf-tests green with the fifteen `[diag-host]` cases on all six lanes (the shipping lanes answering UnknownCommand for fault.* and the recipe case checking that injection is unavailable), including the deliberate-failure application in both modes and the in-process crash-handler case under linux-gcc-asan and linux-clang-tsan, and the two `[diag11a][flight]` cases and the extended `bundle.inspect` cases, crd-ceridc-tests with its `[diag11a]` case and the perf and ceridc fuzz corpora on all six lanes, crd-app-tests and crd-sandbox building on every lane, `crd-diag-coverage` green with the DiagHostStart, DiagHostApply, DiagHostChangeKind and DiagModeState outcome sets, and `crd-package-consumer` green on `win-public-checks` and `linux-gcc-public-checks` with the diagnostic application (symbols `pdb`/`dwarf` reaching the program, 15 `[diag-host]` cases and 16 of the 19 `[diag11a]` cases pass with assertions on all six lanes, `crd-diag-coverage` passes, and `crd-package-consumer` passes on `win-public-checks` (`pdb`) and | 093 | <a id="slice-simd-dispatch"></a>**SIMD-DISPATCH** | Partial | x64 SSE2 (the x86-64 baseline) stays a supported CPU floor through runtime dispatch (user decision 2026-10-26; it replaces DIAG.11b follow-up (r)'s one-AVX2-level plan). All general engine code builds at the baseline ISA. Hot kernels build once per level (SSE2, SSE4.2, AVX2/FMA, AVX-512/AVX10 on x64; NEON and SVE/SVE2 on ARM64 under their own release rows) in isolated kernel units, and one startup selection reads CPUID plus OS-enabled state (XSAVE/XCR0, hybrid cores) with a forced-level override for tests. A guard proves no shared inline body or COMDAT reaches a baseline library from a higher-ISA unit on MSVC, clang-cl, GCC and Clang (the 2026-10-26 LTCG inlining defect). ADR-0063 determinism: declared bit-exact kernels agree across every level, the rest stay within declared tolerances against oracles. One binary runs every level as a forced-level CI arm; AVX-512 kernels are proven under Intel SDE (a download the user approves) until hardware exists. Every affected board (diag-overhead, hesap, geometry, jobs) is re-measured at each level against its peers. **Contract, detection and selection landed** (2026-10-26, [session](sessions/2026-10-26-simd-dispatch-cpu-detection.md)): [ADR-0134](decisions/0134-runtime-cpu-dispatch.md) decides every clause's mechanism; `crd/core/cpu_features.hpp` is the only CPUID/XGETBV site (TickClock reads it), decodes 45 features with their XCR0 state, AVX10 version and width, APX and hybrid core types, intersects every CPU the process may run on and applies `CRD_CPU_LEVEL` (refused above the hardware), judged against the OS view on Windows and Linux with six teeth. **Kernel units landed** (2026-10-26, [session](sessions/2026-10-26-simd-dispatch-kernel-units.md)): `crd_add_cpu_kernels` builds a family once per level in its own object library with that level's flags, `CRD_MATH_LEVEL_NS` keeps every shared inline function per level, `crd/math/kernel_dispatch.hpp` resolves one table from the selection and the build's cap, and the batch small-float conversions are the first family (portable and AVX2 bodies, bit-identical to the scalar converts on every level the host runs, forced-level `crd-math-kernels-at-<level>` CTests, six teeth, board at parity with HEAD). **Level-aware types landed** (2026-10-27, [session](sessions/2026-10-27-simd-dispatch-level-aware-types.md)): `backend.hpp` keys the backend on the unit's level (an SSE4.2 backend; `CRD_SIMD_HAS_*` rungs replace `CRD_SIMD_BACKEND`), every SIMD type, the f64 exp/log cores and their AVX2 twins live in `CRD_MATH_LEVEL_NS` and call no standard-library inline function (`crd/math/libm.hpp`), `kNativeLaneWidth` is 8 on every level, and a 94-operation probe compiled per level proves every level the host runs gives the portable bits (six cross-level defects fixed, eight teeth, linux-gcc-debug too). **Stats kernels landed** (2026-10-27, [session](sessions/2026-10-27-simd-dispatch-hesap-stats-kernels.md)): the Philox and Threefry fills and the Student-t score reduction are the second family, bit-exact on every level (ten teeth, linux-gcc-debug too), the AVX2 fills 1.76x and 1.85x HEAD (Philox 2.1x NumPy), Student-t at HEAD parity and 2.5x JAX; Threefry seek, its wrap carry and Student-t's two formulations fixed; hesap-tensor keeps Philox include-only (ADR-0096 §1). **Left:** the tensor, fft, dense, sparse, special and geometry kernels as per-level units (their raw `__AVX2__` tests go with them), eylem's integrators as kernels before the flip, SSE2/SSE4.2 bodies where they beat the portable loop (a `Vec4d` SSE2 backend, the conversions, the stats family with an exact two-lane `fma` for its log core: portable Student-t is 7.4x behind JAX) and AVX-512 bodies under SDE, NEON parity with the x86 semantics, the baseline flip with the `-sse2` lanes as forced arms, the isolation guard, the level in doctor/host tuple/board records, and the boards per level. | [DIAG.11a](#slice-diag.11a) | [Detail](systems/math-simd.md);
